- **armoctl-integrations**: Outbound integrations — alert channels (Slack/email/webhook), SIEM forwarders, Jira ticket creation. Use to wire ARMO into external workflows. ([armosec/armoctl](https://github.com/armosec/armoctl/tree/HEAD/skills/armoctl-integrations))
- **armoctl-inventory**: Cluster inventory — list workloads, get unique values for a field. Use to enumerate or pivot on resources before applying another command. ([armosec/armoctl](https://github.com/armosec/armoctl/tree/HEAD/skills/armoctl-inventory))

- **armoctl-posture**: Kubernetes posture scanning — controls, frameworks, exceptions. Use when assessing compliance posture (NSA, MITRE, etc.) or managing posture exception policies. ([armosec/armoctl](https://github.com/armosec/armoctl/tree/HEAD/skills/armoctl-posture))
- **armoctl-repo-posture**: Repository posture — IaC scanning of a connected git repo for config issues, with per-file and per-control views. - **armoctl-risks**: Security risks (cross-cutting risk view) — list/resources/severities and exception policies. Use when working with the unified ARMO risk score, not per-domain CVE/posture findings. ([armosec/armoctl](https://github.com/armosec/armoctl/tree/HEAD/skills/armoctl-risks))

- **armoctl-vulns**: ARMO vulnerability triage — list CVEs, find affected images/hosts/workloads, check runtime relevance, manage exception policies. - **arpsych-organizing-framework**: Use when imposing an analytical structure or taxonomy on a psychology literature for an Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) review — the "spine" that turns a reading list into an argument about the field. - **arpsych-proposal-and-commissioning**: Use when getting a topic in front of the Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) Editorial Committee, suggesting a topic/author, or shaping an accepted invitation into an agreed scope. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Annual-Review-of-Psychology-Skills/skills/arpsych-proposal-and-commissioning) · ★1.1k)
- **arpsych-revision**: Use when responding to the Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) Editor's or Committee's peer-review letter on a delivered review, or to copyediting/proof queries. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Annual-Review-of-Psychology-Skills/skills/arpsych-revision) · ★1.1k)
- **arpsych-submission**: Use when running the final delivery preflight for a commissioned Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) review via the Annual Reviews production system — disclosures, reference/format compliance, figure permissions, length, and the transparency of any embedded meta-analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Annual-Review-of-Psychology-Skills/skills/arpsych-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **arpsych-tables-figures**: Use when designing the summary tables and conceptual figures for an Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) review — the who-found-what tables and the one framework diagram that carries the spine. - **arpsych-topic-selection**: Use when judging whether a psychology topic is mature, important, and synthesis-ready enough to warrant an Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) review. - **arpsych-transparency-and-reproducibility**: Use when documenting the literature search and making any embedded meta-analysis reproducible for an Annual Review of Psychology (ARPsych) review.
